<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover">
<title>Black Knight Mine Microgrid Optimizer</title>
<style>
:root{--bg:#f6f7f9;--card:#fff;--tx:#1c2430;--mu:#6a7686;--bd:#d9dee6;--ac:#1f5fbf;--ok:#16794a;--okb:#e0f3e8;--no:#b3261e;--nob:#fbe3e1;box-sizing:border-box;padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}
@media(prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme=light]){--bg:#12161c;--card:#1b222b;--tx:#e6eaf0;--mu:#93a0b1;--bd:#2d3744;--ac:#6aa3ff;--ok:#5fd39a;--okb:#16372a;--no:#ff8a80;--nob:#43201e}}
*{box-sizing:border-box}body{margin:0;background:var(--bg);color:var(--tx);font:14px/1.45 system-ui,-apple-system,"Segoe UI",sans-serif}
main{max-width:1200px;margin:0 auto;padding:16px}h1{font-size:22px;margin:4px 0}h2{font-size:17px;margin:26px 0 10px;border-bottom:2px solid var(--ac);padding-bottom:4px}h3{font-size:13px;margin:0 0 8px;color:var(--mu);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em}
.sub{color:var(--mu)}.grid{display:grid;gap:12px;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(260px,1fr))}
.card{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--bd);border-radius:10px;padding:12px;overflow-x:auto}
label{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;gap:8px;margin:4px 0;font-size:13px}
input[type=number]{width:84px;padding:3px 5px;border:1px solid var(--bd);border-radius:5px;background:var(--bg);color:var(--tx)}
table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:13px}th,td{padding:4px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bd);text-align:right;white-space:nowrap}th:first-child,td:first-child{text-align:left}
button{background:var(--ac);color:#fff;border:0;border-radius:7px;padding:9px 18px;font-size:15px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer}button:disabled{opacity:.5}
.kpi{display:grid;gap:10px;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(150px,1fr))}.kpi div{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--bd);border-radius:8px;padding:8px 10px}.kpi b{display:block;font-size:20px}.kpi span{color:var(--mu);font-size:12px}
.pill{display:inline-block;padding:1px 9px;border-radius:99px;font-weight:700;font-size:12px}.p{background:var(--okb);color:var(--ok)}.f{background:var(--nob);color:var(--no)}
canvas{width:100%;height:260px;display:block}.leg{font-size:12px;margin-top:4px}.leg i{display:inline-block;width:11px;height:11px;margin:0 3px 0 10px;vertical-align:-1px}
#bar{height:6px;background:var(--bd);border-radius:3px;margin-top:8px}#bar div{height:100%;width:0;background:var(--ac);border-radius:3px}
.warn{color:var(--no);font-weight:600}
</style></head><body><main>
<h1>Black Knight Mine Microgrid Optimizer</h1>
<div class="sub">JavaScript port of the uploaded MATLAB dispatch (<code>simulate_microgrid</code>), architecture search (Step 4) and lifecycle-cost (Step 5) logic. Runs entirely in your browser.</div>

<h2>A. Inputs and Assumptions</h2>
<div class="card"><label>Optional: load a different 8760-hour CSV (default data is built in)<input type="file" id="file" accept=".csv"></label><div id="fstat" class="sub">Loading built-in data…</div></div>
<div class="grid" id="inputs" style="margin-top:12px"></div>
<p><button id="run" disabled>Run Optimization</button> <span id="rstat" class="sub"></span></p><div id="bar"><div></div></div>

<div id="out" hidden>
<h2>B. Optimization Results</h2><div id="msg"></div>
<div id="banner"></div><div class="kpi" id="kpi"></div>
<div class="grid" style="margin-top:12px"><div class="card"><h3>Feasibility checks</h3><table id="chk"></table></div>
<div class="card"><h3>Validation vs. expected baseline</h3><div id="valsum"></div><table id="val"></table></div></div>

<h2>C. Visual Results</h2>
<p id="dsn" class="sub"></p><div class="grid" style="grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(480px,1fr))" id="charts"></div>
<h3 style="margin-top:16px">Top 10 feasible configurations by NPV (no reserve requirement; click a row to display it)</h3><div class="card"><table id="top"></table></div>
<h3 style="margin-top:16px">Top 10 reserve-compliant configurations by NPV (tanks sized to meet the reserve; ★ = recommended)</h3><div class="card"><table id="top2"></table></div>
<h2>Model assumptions</h2><div class="card"><ul>
<li>Hourly dispatch of all 8,760 hours, electric and thermal separately, ported from the uploaded <code>simulate_microgrid</code>: renewables → reactor → resistance heat from surplus → battery → diesel, with CHP limits (reactor P+Q ≤ 3,000 kW, diesel P+Q ≤ 2,000 kW, P ≤ 500 kW per unit) and diesel heat-engine heat ≥ electric output.</li>
<li>Microreactor held at 850 kWe per unit in normal operation (85% capacity factor); zero electric and thermal output during the outage. Outage = coldest contiguous 720-hour window; full tanks and batteries at start; no resupply; battery efficiency 100%; diesel 38 MJ/L.</li>
<li>Costs follow the uploaded MATLAB/Excel: OPEX on generated (reactor, diesel) or available (PV, wind) energy, battery and tank fixed OPEX, replacements at each lifetime within the analysis period, discount rate applied to year-end cash flows. Asset counts are integers.</li>
<li>Outage fuel reserve = 100 × final outage fuel inventory ÷ installed tank capacity. Tank count is sized to cover outage fuel use, withdrawal rate and the reserve.</li>
<li><b>The recommended design is selected using a resilience constraint (minimum outage fuel reserve), not pure NPV alone.</b> The explicit reference design is shown as recommended when it is feasible and meets the reserve; the pure lowest-NPV design is always listed separately.</li></ul></div>
</div>

<script>
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s),KWH_GAL=38*3.78541/3.6;
// ---------- parameter schema ----------
const G=[["Assets & search ranges (min / max / step)",[]],];
const ASSET=[["micro","Microreactors",1,1,1],["diesel","Diesel generators",4,4,1],["pv","Solar PV devices",0,3000,100],["wind","Wind turbines",8,14,1],["batt","Batteries",40,160,10],["tank","Diesel tanks (min needed is computed)",1,40,1]];
const PAR=[["Generation specs",[["micro_kw","Micro kWe/device",1000],["micro_tot","Micro max kWth+kWe",3000],["micro_capex","Micro CAPEX $/kW",25000],["micro_opex","Micro OPEX $/kWh",0.08],["micro_life","Micro life yr",10],["micro_frac","Micro output fraction (CF)",0.85],
["diesel_kw","Diesel kWe/device",500],["diesel_tot","Diesel max kWth+kWe",2000],["diesel_capex","Diesel CAPEX $/kW",3500],["diesel_opex","Diesel OPEX $/kWh",1.76],["diesel_life","Diesel life yr",15],
["pv_kw","PV kW/device",1],["pv_capex","PV CAPEX $/kW",3000],["pv_opex","PV OPEX $/kWh",0.01],["pv_life","PV life yr",20],
["wind_kw","Wind kW/device",500],["wind_capex","Wind CAPEX $/kW",2800],["wind_opex","Wind OPEX $/kWh",0.03],["wind_life","Wind life yr",20]]],
["Storage specs",[["batt_kwh","Battery kWh/device",100],["batt_kw","Battery kW/device",50],["batt_capex","Battery CAPEX $/kWh",750],["batt_opex","Battery OPEX $/kWh/yr",7],["batt_life","Battery life yr",10],
["tank_gal","Tank gal/device",10000],["tank_gph","Tank max gal/hr",250],["tank_capex","Tank CAPEX $/tank",100000],["tank_opex","Tank OPEX $/yr",4000],["tank_life","Tank life yr",40]]],
["Financial, load & resource",[["rate","Discount rate",0.06],["years","Analysis period yr",40],["eMult","Electric-load multiplier",1],["tMult","Thermal-load multiplier",1],["pvMult","PV resource multiplier",1],["windMult","Wind resource multiplier",1],["reservePct","Minimum 30-day outage fuel reserve (%)",30]]]];
const P={};const REFD=[["nMicro","Microreactors",1],["nDiesel","Diesel generators",4],["nPV","Solar PV devices",1500],["nWind","Wind turbines",11],["nBatt","Batteries",70],["nTanks","Diesel tanks",4]];
(function(){let h='<div class="card"><h3>Asset search ranges (min / max / step)</h3>';
ASSET.forEach(a=>h+=`<label>${a[1]}<span><input type=number id="r_${a[0]}_0" value=${a[2]}> <input type=number id="r_${a[0]}_1" value=${a[3]}> <input type=number id="r_${a[0]}_2" value=${a[4]}></span></label>`);
h+='</div><div class="card"><h3>Recommended resilience design (explicit reference candidate)</h3>';REFD.forEach(f=>h+=`<label>${f[1]}<input type=number id="ref_${f[0]}" value=${f[2]}></label>`);h+='</div>';PAR.forEach(g=>{h+=`<div class="card"><h3>${g[0]}</h3>`;g[1].forEach(f=>h+=`<label>${f[1]}<input type=number step=any id="p_${f[0]}" value=${f[2]}></label>`);h+='</div>'});$('#inputs').innerHTML=h})();
function readP(){PAR.forEach(g=>g[1].forEach(f=>P[f[0]]=parseFloat($('#p_'+f[0]).value)));
 P.reserve=Math.min(Math.max(P.reservePct/100,0),0.99);const R={};ASSET.forEach(a=>R[a[0]]=[0,1,2].map(i=>parseFloat($(`#r_${a[0]}_${i}`).value)));return R}
const range=(a,b,s)=>{const o=[];s=s>0?s:1;for(let v=a;v<=b+1e-9;v+=s)o.push(Math.round(v));return o.length?o:[a]};
// ---------- data ----------
let RAW=null;
$('#file').onchange=e=>{const f=e.target.files[0];if(!f)return;const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>{try{RAW=parseCSV(r.result);$('#fstat').textContent=`Loaded ${f.name}: ${RAW.n} hourly rows.`;$('#run').disabled=false}catch(x){RAW=null;$('#run').disabled=true;$('#fstat').innerHTML='<span class=warn>'+x.message+'</span>'}};r.readAsText(f)};
function parseCSV(t){const L=t.trim().split(/\r?\n/),H=L[0].split(',').map(s=>s.trim()),need=['hour','temp_F','electric_load_MW','district_heat_MW','pv_cf','wind_cf'],ix={};
 need.forEach(k=>{ix[k]=H.indexOf(k);if(ix[k]<0)throw Error('Missing column: '+k)});const n=L.length-1;if(n!==8760)throw Error('Expected exactly 8760 hourly rows, found '+n);
 const o={n};need.forEach(k=>o[k]=new Float64Array(n));for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const c=L[i+1].split(',');need.forEach(k=>o[k][i]=+c[ix[k]])}return o}
function prep(){const n=RAW.n,D={n,hour:RAW.hour,temp:RAW.temp_F,el:new Float64Array(n),th:new Float64Array(n),pv:new Float64Array(n),wd:new Float64Array(n)};
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++){D.el[i]=RAW.electric_load_MW[i]*1000*P.eMult;D.th[i]=RAW.district_heat_MW[i]*1000*P.tMult;D.pv[i]=Math.min(1,RAW.pv_cf[i]*P.pvMult);D.wd[i]=Math.min(1,RAW.wind_cf[i]*P.windMult)}return D}
function slice(D,s,len){const o={n:len};for(const k of['hour','temp','el','th','pv','wd'])o[k]=D[k].subarray(s,s+len);return o}
function coldest(temp,W){let sum=0;for(let i=0;i<W;i++)sum+=temp[i];let best=sum,bi=0;for(let i=W;i<temp.length;i++){sum+=temp[i]-temp[i-W];if(sum/W<best/W-1e-12){best=sum;bi=i-W+1}}return bi}
// ---------- dispatch (port of simulate_microgrid) ----------
function sim(D,d,o,rec){
 const n=D.n,tol=1e-6,Pm_e=d.nMicro*P.micro_kw,PmT=d.nMicro*P.micro_tot,Pd_e=d.nDiesel*P.diesel_kw,PdT=d.nDiesel*P.diesel_tot,
 pvI=d.nPV*P.pv_kw,wI=d.nWind*P.wind_kw,Eb=d.nBatt*P.batt_kwh,Pb=d.nBatt*P.batt_kw,fuelCap=d.nTanks*P.tank_gal,flowMax=d.nTanks*P.tank_gph;
 let soc=Math.min(Math.max(o.soc0*Eb,0),Eb),inv=Math.min(Math.max(o.fuel0*fuelCap,0),fuelCap);const soc0=soc;
 const A=rec?['pvE','wE','mE','mH','resH','dis','ch','dE','dH','soc','inv','flag','load','hload'].reduce((a,k)=>(a[k]=new Float64Array(n),a),{}):null;
 const S={pvAv:0,wAv:0,mGen:0,dEl:0,dTot:0,uE:0,uH:0,fuel:0,maxFuelHr:0,minSOC:1e18,maxSOC:-1e18,maxCh:0,maxDis:0,maxDE:0,maxDT:0,minInv:1e18,maxInv:-1e18,resH:0};
 for(let t=0;t<n;t++){const E=D.el[t],H=D.th[t],Ppv=pvI*D.pv[t],Pw=wI*D.wd[t],Pr=Ppv+Pw;S.pvAv+=Ppv;S.wAv+=Pw;
  const nMA=d.nMicro*(o.micro?1:0),Pm=nMA*P.micro_kw*P.micro_frac;
  const prE=Math.min(Pr,E);let Er=E-prE;const mE=Math.min(Pm,Er),curt=Pm-mE;Er-=mE;
  const Qm=Math.max(0,nMA*P.micro_tot-Pm),mH=Math.min(Qm,H);let Hr=H-mH;
  const sur=Pr-prE+curt,rH=Math.min(sur,Hr);Hr-=rH;const after=sur-rH;
  let ch=0;if(Eb>0){ch=Math.min(after,Pb,Math.max(0,Eb-soc));soc+=ch}
  let dis=0;if(Eb>0){dis=Math.min(Er,Pb,soc);soc-=dis}Er-=dis;
  const lim=o.enf?Math.min(inv*KWH_GAL,flowMax*KWH_GAL):Infinity;
  const dE=Math.min(Er,Pd_e,PdT,lim/2);Er-=dE;const dH=Math.min(Hr,Math.max(0,PdT-dE),Math.max(0,lim-dE));Hr-=dH;
  const tot=dE+Math.max(dH,dE);let fu=tot/KWH_GAL;if(o.enf){fu=Math.min(fu,inv);inv=Math.max(0,inv-fu)}
  S.mGen+=Pm;S.dEl+=dE;S.dTot+=tot;S.uE+=Math.max(0,Er);S.uH+=Math.max(0,Hr);S.fuel+=fu;S.resH+=rH;
  if(fu>S.maxFuelHr)S.maxFuelHr=fu;S.minSOC=Math.min(S.minSOC,soc);S.maxSOC=Math.max(S.maxSOC,soc);S.maxCh=Math.max(S.maxCh,ch);S.maxDis=Math.max(S.maxDis,dis);S.maxDE=Math.max(S.maxDE,dE);S.maxDT=Math.max(S.maxDT,tot);S.minInv=Math.min(S.minInv,inv);S.maxInv=Math.max(S.maxInv,inv);
  if(rec){const k=pvI+wI>1e-9&&Pr>1e-9?prE/Pr:0;A.pvE[t]=k*Ppv;A.wE[t]=k*Pw;A.mE[t]=mE;A.mH[t]=mH;A.resH[t]=rH;A.dis[t]=dis;A.ch[t]=ch;A.dE[t]=dE;A.dH[t]=dH;A.soc[t]=soc;A.inv[t]=inv;A.flag[t]=tot>1e-9?1:0;A.load[t]=E;A.hload[t]=H}}
 S.avail=o.micro?n*d.nMicro:0;S.cf=d.nMicro>0&&S.avail>0?S.mGen/(P.micro_kw*S.avail):NaN;
 const c={elec:S.uE/1000<=tol,heat:S.uH/1000<=tol,batt:S.minSOC>=-tol&&S.maxSOC<=Eb+tol&&S.maxCh<=Pb+tol&&S.maxDis<=Pb+tol,
  endSOC:o.endSOC?soc>=soc0-tol:true,dsl:S.maxDE<=Pd_e+tol,chp:S.maxDT<=PdT+tol,
  inv:o.enf?S.minInv>=-tol&&S.maxInv<=fuelCap+tol:true,flow:o.enf?S.maxFuelHr<=flowMax+tol:true,cf:o.cf&&d.nMicro>0&&S.avail>0?S.cf>=P.micro_frac-tol:true};
 S.checks=c;S.feasible=Object.values(c).every(Boolean);S.final=inv;S.finalSOC=soc;S.fuelCap=fuelCap;S.d=d;S.A=A;return S}
const OA={soc0:0,fuel0:1,enf:false,cf:true,endSOC:true,micro:true},OO={soc0:1,fuel0:1,enf:true,cf:false,endSOC:false,micro:false};
// ---------- lifecycle cost (port of calculate_lifecycle_cost) ----------
function repPV(cap,life,N,r){if(cap<=0)return 0;let s=0;for(let y=life;y<=N-1;y+=life)s+=cap/Math.pow(1+r,y);return s}
function cost(d,A){const c={micro:d.nMicro*P.micro_kw*P.micro_capex,diesel:d.nDiesel*P.diesel_kw*P.diesel_capex,pv:d.nPV*P.pv_kw*P.pv_capex,wind:d.nWind*P.wind_kw*P.wind_capex,batt:d.nBatt*P.batt_kwh*P.batt_capex,tank:d.nTanks*P.tank_capex};
 const capex=Object.values(c).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
 const opex=A.mGen*P.micro_opex+A.dTot*P.diesel_opex+A.pvAv*P.pv_opex+A.wAv*P.wind_opex+d.nBatt*P.batt_kwh*P.batt_opex+d.nTanks*P.tank_opex;
 let pvo=0;for(let y=1;y<=P.years;y++)pvo+=opex/Math.pow(1+P.rate,y);
 let rep=0;for(const k of['micro','diesel','pv','wind','batt','tank'])rep+=repPV(c[k],P[k+'_life'],P.years,P.rate);
 return{capex,opex,npv:capex+pvo+rep}}
// ---------- search (port of Step 4 + resilience tank sizing) ----------
async function optimize(D,Do,R){const T=[];for(const m of range(...R.micro))for(const dg of range(...R.diesel))for(const w of range(...R.wind))for(const p of range(...R.pv))for(const b of range(...R.batt))T.push([m,dg,w,p,b]);
 const res=[],pure=[];const t0=performance.now();
 for(let i=0;i<T.length;i++){const[m,dg,w,p,b]=T[i],d={nMicro:m,nDiesel:dg,nWind:w,nPV:p,nBatt:b,nTanks:20};const a=sim(D,d,OA,false);
  if(a.feasible){const o=sim(Do,d,OO,false);if(o.feasible){
   let base=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(o.fuel/P.tank_gal-1e-9),Math.ceil(o.maxFuelHr/P.tank_gph-1e-9)),rt=Math.max(base,Math.ceil(o.fuel/(P.tank_gal*(1-P.reserve))-1e-9));if(dg===0)base=rt=0;
   for(const[nt0,list]of[[base,pure],[rt,res]]){const nt=Math.max(nt0,R.tank[0]);if(nt>R.tank[1])continue;const d2={...d,nTanks:nt},o2=sim(Do,d2,OO,false);
    if(o2.feasible&&(list===pure||o2.final>=P.reserve*o2.fuelCap-1e-6))list.push(entry(d2,a,o2))}}}
  if(i%40===0){$('#bar div').style.width=(100*i/T.length)+'%';$('#rstat').textContent=`Screened ${i}/${T.length} configurations…`;await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r))}}
 res.sort((x,y)=>x.npv-y.npv);pure.sort((x,y)=>x.npv-y.npv);return{res,pure,total:T.length,sec:(performance.now()-t0)/1000}}
function entry(d,a,o){const c=cost(d,a),cap=o.fuelCap;return{d:{...d},...c,fuel:o.fuel,fin:o.final,pct:cap?o.final/cap:1}}
function evalRef(D,Do,d){const a=sim(D,d,OA,false),o=sim(Do,d,OO,false),e=entry(d,a,o);e.ok=a.feasible&&o.feasible&&e.pct>=P.reserve-1e-9;e.feas=a.feasible&&o.feasible;return e}
// ---------- UI ----------
const f0=(v,d=0)=>v.toLocaleString(undefined,{minimumFractionDigits:d,maximumFractionDigits:d}),M=v=>'$'+f0(v/1e6,2)+'M',pill=ok=>`<span class="pill ${ok?'p':'f'}">${ok?'PASS':'FAIL'}</span>`;
const ds=d=>`${d.nMicro} micro / ${d.nDiesel} diesel / ${f0(d.nPV)} PV / ${d.nWind} wind / ${d.nBatt} batt / ${d.nTanks} tanks`;
let CTX=null;
$('#run').onclick=async()=>{const btn=$('#run');btn.disabled=true;try{const R=readP(),D=prep(),W=720,st=coldest(D.temp,W),Do=slice(D,st,W);
  const o=await optimize(D,Do,R);$('#bar div').style.width='100%';$('#rstat').textContent=`Done: ${o.total} configurations in ${o.sec.toFixed(1)} s; ${o.pure.length} feasible, ${o.res.length} also meet the ${f0(100*P.reserve)}% reserve.`;$('#out').hidden=false;
  const ref={};REFD.forEach(f=>ref[f[0]]=parseFloat($('#ref_'+f[0]).value));const rec=evalRef(D,Do,ref);
  let recE=null,src='';if(rec.ok){recE=rec;src=`Reference candidate ${ds(ref)} was evaluated explicitly; it passes all annual and outage constraints and meets the ${f0(100*P.reserve)}% reserve threshold.`}
  else if(o.res.length){recE=o.res[0];src=`The reference candidate ${ds(ref)} ${rec.feas?'does not meet the reserve threshold':'is infeasible'}, so the lowest-NPV design in the reserve-compliant set is recommended instead.`}
  if(!recE){$('#kpi').innerHTML='';$('#banner').innerHTML='';$('#msg').innerHTML='<p class=warn>No feasible reserve-compliant design in the search ranges. Widen the ranges or lower the reserve.</p>';return}
  $('#msg').innerHTML='';CTX={D,Do,st,W,res:o.res,pure:o.pure,rec:recE,src,ref};show({k:'rec'})}catch(e){$('#rstat').innerHTML='<span class=warn>'+e.message+'</span>';console.error(e)}btn.disabled=false};
function pick(k,i){show({k,i});scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'})}
function show(sel){const{D,Do,st,W,res,pure,rec,src}=CTX,h0=D.hour[st],h1=D.hour[st+W-1];
 const E=sel.k==='rec'?rec:sel.k==='p'?pure[sel.i]:res[sel.i],lab=sel.k==='rec'?'Recommended Resilience-Adjusted Architecture':sel.k==='p'?`Lowest-NPV list, rank ${sel.i+1}`:`Reserve-compliant list, rank ${sel.i+1}`;
 const d=E.d,a=sim(D,d,OA,true),o=sim(Do,d,OO,true),cs=cost(d,a);
 // banners
 const lo=pure[0],rk=res.findIndex(x=>ds(x.d)===ds(rec.d))+1,cheaper=res.length&&res[0].npv<rec.npv-1;
 $('#banner').innerHTML=`<div class="grid"><div class="card"><h3>(a) Lowest-NPV Feasible Architecture</h3>${lo?`<b>${ds(lo.d)}</b><br>NPV ${M(lo.npv)} · CAPEX ${M(lo.capex)} · fuel reserve ${f0(100*lo.pct,1)}%<br><span class=sub>Pure NPV rank 1; sized with the minimum tanks and no reserve requirement.</span>`:'none'}</div>
 <div class="card" style="border:2px solid var(--ok)"><h3>(b) Recommended Resilience-Adjusted Architecture</h3><b>${ds(rec.d)}</b><br>NPV ${M(rec.npv)} · CAPEX ${M(rec.capex)} · OPEX ${M(rec.opex)}/yr<br><span style="font-size:22px;font-weight:700;color:var(--ok)">Outage fuel reserve: ${f0(100*rec.pct,2)}%</span> <span class=sub>(minimum ${f0(100*P.reserve)}%)</span></div></div>
 <p class=sub><b>Selection note:</b> the recommended design is selected using a resilience constraint (minimum 30-day outage fuel reserve), not pure NPV alone. ${src} ${rk?`Among reserve-compliant designs it ranks ${rk} of ${res.length} by NPV`:'It is outside the searched reserve-compliant list'}${cheaper?`; the cheapest reserve-compliant alternative is ${ds(res[0].d)} at ${M(res[0].npv)}, which is ${M(rec.npv-res[0].npv).replace('$','$')} lower in NPV.`:'.'}</p><p><b>Displayed below:</b> ${lab} — ${ds(d)}</p>`;
 const K=(l,v)=>`<div><span>${l}</span><b>${v}</b></div>`;
 $('#kpi').innerHTML=K('Microreactors',d.nMicro)+K('Diesel generators',d.nDiesel)+K('Solar PV devices',f0(d.nPV))+K('Wind turbines',d.nWind)+K('Batteries',d.nBatt)+K('Diesel tanks',d.nTanks)+
  K('CAPEX',M(cs.capex))+K('Annual OPEX',M(cs.opex)+'/yr')+K(P.years+'-yr NPV',M(cs.npv))+K('Annual diesel use',f0(a.fuel)+' gal')+K('Outage diesel use',f0(o.fuel)+' gal')+K('Final outage fuel',f0(o.final)+' gal')+K('Outage fuel reserve',f0(100*o.final/o.fuelCap,2)+'%')+K('Outage window','Hr '+h0+'–'+h1);
 const ac=a.checks,oc=o.checks,rsv=o.final>=P.reserve*o.fuelCap-1e-6,rows=[['Annual electricity served',ac.elec],['Annual heat served',ac.heat],['Outage electricity served',oc.elec],['Outage heat served',oc.heat],['Battery limits (SOC & power)',ac.batt&&oc.batt],['Diesel limits (≤500 kWe/unit)',ac.dsl&&oc.dsl],['CHP limits (P+Q ≤ 2,000 kW/unit)',ac.chp&&oc.chp],['Fuel inventory within bounds',oc.inv],['Fuel withdrawal rate',oc.flow],['Microreactor capacity factor ≥ '+P.micro_frac,ac.cf],['Outage fuel reserve ≥ '+f0(100*P.reserve)+'%',rsv]];
 $('#chk').innerHTML=rows.map(r=>`<tr><td>${r[0]}</td><td>${pill(r[1])}</td></tr>`).join('')+`<tr><td>Annual microreactor CF</td><td>${a.cf.toFixed(3)}</td></tr>`;
 // validation: ALWAYS against the recommended design (independent of the clicked row)
 const rd=rec.d,a2=sim(D,rd,OA,false),o2=sim(Do,rd,OO,false),c2=cost(rd,a2),eD=D.el.reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0)/1000,tD=D.th.reduce((x,y)=>x+y,0)/1000,pkE=Math.max(...D.el)/1000,pkT=Math.max(...D.th)/1000;
 const V=[['Annual electrical demand (MWh)',15532.5,eD,.002],['Annual thermal demand (MWh-th)',9347.4,tD,.002],['Peak electric load (MW)',2.034,pkE,.002],['Peak thermal load (MWth)',2.118,pkT,.002],['Outage start hour',418,h0,0],['Outage end hour',1137,h1,0],
 ['Recommended: microreactors',1,rd.nMicro,0],['Recommended: diesel generators',4,rd.nDiesel,0],['Recommended: PV devices',1500,rd.nPV,0],['Recommended: wind turbines',11,rd.nWind,0],['Recommended: batteries',70,rd.nBatt,0],['Recommended: diesel tanks',4,rd.nTanks,0],
 ['Recommended: CAPEX ($M)',57.55,c2.capex/1e6,.005],['Recommended: annual OPEX ($M/yr)',1.70,c2.opex/1e6,.005],['Recommended: NPV ($M)',125.10,c2.npv/1e6,.005],['Recommended: outage fuel used (gal)',26023,o2.fuel,.002],['Recommended: outage fuel remaining (gal)',13977,o2.final,.002],['Recommended: outage fuel reserve (%)',34.94,100*o2.final/o2.fuelCap,.002],
 ['Recommended: annual unserved electric (MWh)',0,a2.uE/1000,0],['Recommended: annual unserved thermal (MWh-th)',0,a2.uH/1000,0],['Recommended: outage unserved electric (MWh)',0,o2.uE/1000,0],['Recommended: outage unserved thermal (MWh-th)',0,o2.uH/1000,0]];
 let bad=0;const rowv=(n,e,v,ok,dec)=>{if(!ok)bad++;return`<tr><td>${n}</td><td>${e}</td><td>${v}</td><td>${pill(ok)}</td></tr>`};
 let vr=V.map(v=>rowv(v[0],v[1],f0(v[2],v[1]<10&&v[1]!==0?3:v[2]%1?2:0),Math.abs(v[2]-v[1])<=Math.max(v[3]*Math.abs(v[1]),1e-6)));
 const meets=a2.feasible&&o2.feasible&&o2.final>=P.reserve*o2.fuelCap-1e-6;
 vr.push(rowv('Recommended design meets minimum outage fuel reserve (%)','PASS at ≥ '+f0(100*P.reserve)+'%',f0(100*o2.final/o2.fuelCap,2)+'%',meets));
 const base=P.eMult===1&&P.tMult===1&&P.pvMult===1&&P.windMult===1&&Math.abs(P.reserve-0.3)<1e-9;
 $('#valsum').innerHTML=(bad?`<p class=warn>${bad} of ${V.length+1} checks do not match the expected baseline.${base?'':' (Non-baseline multipliers/reserve are set, so mismatches are expected.)'}</p>`:`<p style="color:var(--ok);font-weight:600">All ${V.length+1} baseline checks PASS.</p>`)+
  `<p class=sub><b>Informational:</b> lowest-NPV feasible design ${lo?ds(lo.d)+' ('+M(lo.npv)+', rank 1)':'n/a'}. Recommended design NPV rank: ${rk||'n/a'} of ${res.length} reserve-compliant designs${(()=>{const i=pure.findIndex(x=>ds(x.d)===ds(rec.d));return i>=0?'; '+(i+1)+' of '+pure.length+' among all feasible designs':''})()}.</p>`;
 $('#val').innerHTML='<tr><th>Metric</th><th>Expected</th><th>Dashboard</th><th></th></tr>'+vr.join('');
 // charts
 const C=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement),tx=C.getPropertyValue('--tx'),bd=C.getPropertyValue('--bd'),k=1/1000;
 const hrs=Array.from(D.hour),oh=Array.from(Do.hour),sc=arr=>Array.from(arr,v=>v*k);
 const ch=[['Annual electrical dispatch (MW)',hrs,[['Solar PV','#f2b705',sc(a.A.pvE)],['Wind','#3aa5d9',sc(a.A.wE)],['Microreactor','#8a4fd0',sc(a.A.mE)],['Battery discharge','#2e9e5b',sc(a.A.dis)],['Diesel','#d6453d',sc(a.A.dE)]],sc(a.A.load),'Electric load'],
 ['Annual thermal dispatch (MWth)',hrs,[['Microreactor CHP','#8a4fd0',sc(a.A.mH)],['Resistance heat (PV/wind)','#e07b00',sc(a.A.resH)],['Diesel CHP','#d6453d',sc(a.A.dH)]],sc(a.A.hload),'Thermal load'],
 ['30-day outage electrical dispatch (MW)',oh,[['Solar PV','#f2b705',sc(o.A.pvE)],['Wind','#3aa5d9',sc(o.A.wE)],['Battery discharge','#2e9e5b',sc(o.A.dis)],['Diesel','#d6453d',sc(o.A.dE)]],sc(o.A.load),'Electric load'],
 ['30-day outage thermal dispatch (MWth)',oh,[['Resistance heat (PV/wind)','#e07b00',sc(o.A.resH)],['Diesel CHP','#d6453d',sc(o.A.dH)]],sc(o.A.hload),'Thermal load'],
 ['Battery state of charge, outage (MWh)',oh,[['Battery SOC','#2e9e5b',sc(o.A.soc)]],null],
 ['Diesel fuel inventory, outage (thousand gal)',oh,[['Fuel inventory','#d6453d',sc(o.A.inv)]],null]];
 $('#dsn').innerHTML=`Charts show: <b>${lab}</b> — ${ds(d)}`;
 $('#charts').innerHTML=ch.map((c,i)=>`<div class="card"><h3>${c[0]}</h3><canvas id="cv${i}"></canvas><div class="leg">${c[2].map(s=>`<i style="background:${s[1]}"></i>${s[0]}`).join('')}${c[3]?`<i style="background:${tx}"></i>${c[4]}`:''}</div></div>`).join('');
 ch.forEach((c,i)=>draw($('#cv'+i),c[1],c[2],c[3],tx,bd));
 const hd='<tr><th>#</th><th>Micro</th><th>Diesel</th><th>PV</th><th>Wind</th><th>Batt</th><th>Tanks</th><th>CAPEX</th><th>OPEX/yr</th><th>NPV</th><th>Outage fuel gal</th><th>Reserve</th><th>Note</th></tr>';
 const tb=(list,kk)=>hd+list.slice(0,10).map((r,i)=>{const isRec=ds(r.d)===ds(rec.d),cur=sel.k===kk&&sel.i===i;return`<tr style="cursor:pointer;${cur?'font-weight:700;':''}${isRec?'background:var(--okb)':''}" onclick="pick('${kk}',${i})"><td>${i+1}</td><td>${r.d.nMicro}</td><td>${r.d.nDiesel}</td><td>${f0(r.d.nPV)}</td><td>${r.d.nWind}</td><td>${r.d.nBatt}</td><td>${r.d.nTanks}</td><td>${M(r.capex)}</td><td>${M(r.opex)}</td><td>${M(r.npv)}</td><td>${f0(r.fuel)}</td><td>${f0(100*r.pct,1)}%</td><td>${(kk==='p'&&i===0?'Lowest-NPV feasible design ':'')+(isRec?'★ Recommended':'')}</td></tr>`}).join('');
 $('#top').innerHTML=tb(pure,'p');$('#top2').innerHTML=tb(res,'r')}
function draw(cv,x,S,line,tx,bd){const dpr=devicePixelRatio||1,w=cv.clientWidth,h=cv.clientHeight;cv.width=w*dpr;cv.height=h*dpr;const g=cv.getContext('2d');g.scale(dpr,dpr);
 const n=x.length,L=44,B=22,T=6,cum=new Float64Array(n);let mx=0;const tops=S.map(s=>{const t=new Float64Array(n);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){cum[i]+=s[2][i];t[i]=cum[i]}return t});
 for(let i=0;i<n;i++)mx=Math.max(mx,cum[i],line?line[i]:0);mx=mx*1.08||1;const X=i=>L+(w-L-6)*i/(n-1),Y=v=>T+(h-T-B)*(1-v/mx);
 g.font='11px system-ui';g.fillStyle=tx;g.strokeStyle=bd;g.textAlign='right';for(let k=0;k<=4;k++){const v=mx*k/4,y=Y(v);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(L,y);g.lineTo(w-6,y);g.stroke();g.fillText(v.toFixed(mx<10?2:0),L-4,y+4)}
 g.textAlign='center';for(let k=0;k<=5;k++){const i=Math.round((n-1)*k/5);g.fillText(x[i],X(i),h-6)}
 for(let s=S.length-1;s>=0;s--){g.fillStyle=S[s][1];g.globalAlpha=.85;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(X(0),Y(0));for(let i=0;i<n;i++)g.lineTo(X(i),Y(tops[s][i]));g.lineTo(X(n-1),Y(0));g.closePath();g.fill()}g.globalAlpha=1;
 if(line){g.strokeStyle=tx;g.lineWidth=1.2;g.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<n;i++)i?g.lineTo(X(i),Y(line[i])):g.moveTo(X(i),Y(line[i]));g.stroke()}}
